[06/04/2025 6:27 pm] iamjustwhiteeye
Honestly bro tbh you just sound like you're spoiled. 😭 I'm not trying to say you are, nor dissing you. You have no complete reason to hate your life, just like a lot of people, but as human beings we give reasons. Reasons to hate it, reasons to not care or like it. We validate it even when it's not real. Ofc it's real but the validation isn't. Your bored but do you know completely why your bored, have you thought well where does it come from, beyond not having anything to do or friends, where does it reside and where does the thoughts stem from? Self awareness/perception. Having the metacognition. Think about a rich girl hating their mom because their supra was green instead of their favorite color blue. In our minds it will be their spoiled. But in their head it's as if their mom didn't care enough, as If she just had a vendetta. You get to used to something, that when something of high expectation doesn't come, it makes you react emotionally according to how you are. I remember when I was a kid, literally would be left home for hours on end, no food in the house, my sister comes home, she has food but I don't. I was forgotten about, I was mad, I was sad, why did my sister get something, why did they not think of me? But everytime it happened, I just understood that you can't expect to be given anything. 

I wasn't given glamor, I was given a life though, my life was supposed to worse, I was supposed to be brain dead by now, unable to walk, but I'm doing this with you. Grace. Do you have grace? The whole point of all this is just grace. Once you start appreciating even the little things, the things that show you it isn't that bad and you have that freedom of doing more, then it transforms boredom to something more. Maybe it doesn't but everything else doesn't matter because at least you have this. You can want, you can desire, you can be prideful, you can be greedy, but knowing and understanding that you have something, or at least being grateful for it make things a lot better.

[07/04/2025 8:44 am] iamjustwhiteeye
You should do intermediate fasting instead of just doing one meal a day. Yeah you'll shed body fat by eating less calories and burning a lot more. But in that same notion you're losing muscle, and not giving enough nutrients for your body to grow. The thing is people usually get into this mindset where they want one thing so they'll go to the extreme to get it, or rationalize that because I do this, this is more likely to happen without actually thinking about other factors as well.

Instead of just going on the scale, measure your body with measuring tape. Like this:

Measurements (Precision Format):

Core Stats
• Height:
• Current Weight:
• Body Fat %:

Upper Body
• Neck circumference (inches/cm):
• Arm length:
• Shoulder width (acromion to acromion):
• Chest circumference (at nipple line):
• Upper arm (flexed):
• Forearm (relaxed):
• Wrist circumference:

Torso
• Waist circumference (at navel):
• Stomach circumference (widest point if different):
• Back width (armpit to armpit or scapula to scapula):

Lower Body
• Hip circumference (widest point):
• Leg length:
• Thigh circumference (mid-point):
• Calf circumference:
• Ankle circumference:

Compare and contrast, nostalgia and reminiscence. All good until you use that as a basis of want you want your life to be, that's terrible transitional energy because you need to look forward to see what's ahead, only look back to learn what you missed.

[13/05/2025 10:51 pm] iamjustwhiteeye
Also. He's your father that is honorable and understands the joy of life/stress of it also. You can always do better, should strive for better, but learn to enjoy your achievements and W's. My father also told me why settle for a 80 when you can push for a 90, but he will always tell me good job when I get a high grade and be proud of me for being able to do that.

 It's simple. It's just natural gratitude. A 87 is honestly really good, but what if that person been getting 95's their whole life, it would feel as if the world is crashing, compared to someone that been getting 80's and high 70's, they'll be feeling euphoria. The difference is standards, and how you percieve them, and based on those standards your vision will only celebrate and grant gratitude for a success that you don't deem possible, and for someone who been at those times again and again, it's just common. 

You can see this in all things, a spoiled brat would get mad for getting the wrong colored sports car, while a person in lower economy would be in so much joy getting something like a snack that they enjoy. Or when someone that's constantly pushed as ugly gets a compliment, they're feeling anxious but also happy, while the person who've been praised all their life just make it seem like the casual, an expectation. At 9 I understood that expectation ruin the world, but that's all we can do. Expect, predict and believe in a future that's not there yet. It's a gift and a curse. The greater the expectation and belief, the worse the damage is when it isn't granted.

[14/05/2025 10:28 pm] iamjustwhiteeye
Just copy and paste in the journal.

Group 1: Self-Reflection & Internal Change

Then tell yourself what changed about you that enables that?

What enables certain actions to happen for their reason?

What emotions do you feel once it happen?

Why does it matter as much?

Does it change your world drastically?



---

Group 2: Friendship & Meaning

What is actually friends?

How were they your friends?

What do you think makes you a friend to them?

What is a true friend?

What is something you noticed?


Group 3: Disappointment & Emotional Depth

What is something you believe they had that makes it disappointing?

Why do you feel only disappointed and nothing else?

Why do you care emotionally?

How did they change your life or how you see yourself?

Who is the people around you?


Group 4: Enjoyment & Life

Why do you enjoy it?

What do you enjoy?

How do you enjoy life?

What is enjoyment?

What creates enjoyment in life?

When feeling enjoyment what do you experience?

How do you incorporate enjoyment in your life?


Group 5: Friendship Revisited

How do you see the people that are your friends?

What are friends to you?

Why is they your friend?

When did they become so close to you?

[14/05/2025 10:43 pm] iamjustwhiteeye
See you need to increase your awareness. Question answers with more questions, break down your answers, explain deeper and fully. Don't go for a simple answer, even when the answer is simple, tell why is it simple for you. Learn to ask more deeper reflective questions, so you can fully realize yourself internally. 

Don't do it how I do it, because I am responding to you, so for me I'd just give you questions to what you said. 

You can even question what I just said, how did I realize it, what awareness did I have over my question and why. 

You see how I interacted then questioned it and gave you something else to look at. That's what you have to work on. 

If I'm answering something like what is loneliness? I would say my answer, then expand on my answer with a answer to another question about some parts of it. If I were to say. 

[Answer] Loneliness to me is just the act of being in a black void, where nothing is around you but everything you can see everything [answer question] When I think of a black void in accordance to loneliness, what i mean is a feeling of emptiness, a part of you not being completely present. Because loneliness isn't just being there, it's being there and realizing everything you see isn't there with you. [Answer question] I think a lot of people exprience loneliness because of not being with themselves a lot, or not seeing themselves as comfort. When you're lonely, you don't have anyone and unfortunately you feel like you don't have you either. It's dark, while bright because it's a illusion at the end of the day. [Answer question] an illusion that shadows that brightness, make you feel uneasy, like many things don't matter because they don't interact with you. Why should it matter if you don't even acknowledge it in your existence. And what happens when you don't believe you exist. It's fucked, but to get out of it, you have to realize that you're only alone internally, externally you still have other, you just don't reach out. But it also doesn't matter whether connection happens or not, it's only a matter if you value it. [Answer question] and when I say that, connection is everything, I feel connected to the trees, the world around me, because I know I'm not lonely or alone, I just am. I'm me. I'm the only one who is me. And I can only value what's infront of me, not what others value infront of them.

[15/05/2025 10:57 pm] iamjustwhiteeye
But bro, I literally accounted for all the details needed for your plan. 1860-1890 isn't a insane defecit that'll stunt growth. Over months you'll lose fat while gaining more muscle, and because youre active your in a higher defecit range anyway. 

Just think about your original weight being the bulk of the lifetime then coming all the down to a lower bf % gaining muscle mass, strength, agility all at once (which you get more benefits to being more overweight, because you need to do more work and physically it's harder). Because of being so active as a 16 year old, peaking into your growth, you'll be better off because your testorone increases (especially if you consistently drink at least 4L of water everyday, hydration is important) blood flow maximizes, and your natural energy start to flowing. 

I wouldnt even be mad if you were like, hey imma just do 1650-1750, I'll be like ok but understand how to get the amount of protein you need. Counting macros like a mad lad. Throughout this process you skip a lot of levels that youll need to do instead of what youre doing, working out your muscles, doing so much cardio, without much fuel to replenish and grow so you can maximize strength and while losing fat. 

Then you can go up in weight (equipment weight) lift heavier, having increase load, giving more strength at a lower bf and body weight, effectively creating a chain where even though you lower your bf and weight, you gain a lot more strength due to maximizing on the hidden benefits of being heavier. Youll be a fucking unit basically. Once you go down to 12%, you can bulk up to 25% lifting heavier, doing more endurance while on load (weight or intensity), which fills your muscle, increases your muscle mass while going up, so when you cut back down to 12% you are absolutely jacked. Mix everything with animalistic movements, mobility drills, agility training. Your stats and body will be maxed out, being capable of doing a lot of things physically with great strength and power + endurance. 

I do this because I care about your long term then short term. Im cool with you doing your thing but sometimes just listen to me because im looking out for you 100%. The stuff im saying and doing is tailored to who you are and what you want. So at the end of the day, that day im not here, your life can be beautiful and amazing, better then you ever had.

[29/07/2025 6:39 am] iamjustwhiteeye
Yes, the body does use fat for energy initially. But when you're in a consistent calorie deficit, especially a drastic one, your body eventually runs out of easily accessible energy stores. And even if you’re training, once glycogen is depleted and dietary protein isn’t enough to meet demands, your body starts tapping into lean tissue — muscle — for fuel.

Why? Because muscle is metabolically expensive. The body prioritizes survival, not aesthetics or performance. So even the muscles you do use aren’t safe forever. If you're underfed long enough, the body will start breaking down muscle protein to convert into glucose to fuel your brain and organs.

And then after that. Once your deprived, your body shifts from recovery to survival. As i said you're only the consciousness of the body, not the body itself

Starvation doesn’t care about your training split it’s pure biology 💀. Your body’s just trying to make it to the next meal. Doesn’t matter if you lifted or ran. If your intake doesn’t match your energy and protein demands, it’ll eat the muscle eventually.
